- **Phase 6 Goal 6-5 (Control-IN / Natural-Language Primary) — ✅ COMPLETE 2026-07-21 (`a9c1d9a`): `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN` FLIPPED DEFAULT-ON, all 6 checklist items closed, the deployed image is TWO-WAY. **SCOPE OF "COMPLETE": the SECURITY arc is complete and the channel is live; ONE §14 done-when is NOT met and is carried forward — the canon *multi-turn conversation referencing a fact from a PRIOR control-IN session*. That is a FUNCTIONALITY gap, not a testing gap: `pa_ctrl_gate` deliberately CLEARS the retrieval preamble for a control-IN query (`main_x86.c:2670-2672` — "retrieval-grounded control-IN is a later slice"), so the recall half is UNWIRED (the `EPI_ACT_CONTROL_IN` episodic WRITE half IS proven). Second carry-forward — **COMMENT CORRECTED IN PLACE 2026-07-26 (comment-only, zero behaviour change; `main.c.obj` `.text`/`.rodata`/`.data` + `nm` byte-identical, DWARF-only whole-file delta).** `main_x86.c`'s **`spine_decide()`** (cite the SYMBOL — the old citation `main_x86.c:2164` had ROTTED, 2164 is now inside the control-IN input-process spawn) passes `control_in_available=false` with what used to read "no control-IN yet". That comment went FALSE at the 6-5 flip (control-IN default-ON since 2026-07-21) — **but the `false` literal is still CORRECT and inert, and was deliberately NOT changed**: that argument is read by exactly ONE branch of `trust_policy` (`TRUST_REQUEST`, `shield_action.c`), and `action_allowlist.c` holds exactly **FOUR** entries, all `TRUST_AUTO` or `TRUST_NOTIFY` — no `TRUST_REQUEST`, no `TRUST_REQUIRE` — so the branch is **unreachable in the deployed build**. Wiring it to a live value would ALSO break `spine_decide`'s documented **"Pure: touches ZERO globals"** property, which was a deliberate outcome of the 6-1/M1 behaviour-neutral extract — a real design decision, not a tidy-up. The replacement comment states why the literal is inert AND what to wire it to when the first `TRUST_REQUEST` action lands: the same channel-up gate `TLM_F_CONTROL_IN` uses (`g_ctrl_key_ok && g_ctrl_floor_ok && g_ctrl_console_ok && g_ctrl_rx_ready`), with the purity property revisited at that point.** This was the SECURITY LONG POLE and the LAST hard-security goal of the phase; next = 6-6 (routing ≥95%) / 6-7 (the 7-day supervised exit).** `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-5_CONTROL_IN.md` — authored 2026-07-13 (post-6-3 next goal — the phase's hardest security gate: it turns the read-only telemetry console two-way and opens the box to the FIRST untrusted inbound it has ever accepted). Canon (`ROADMAP.md:90`): "conversation is the default interface … gated on the full security checklist: auth + HMAC, real SHIELD (close SEC-039), rate-limiting, a hardened/fuzzed inbound parser, and ideally a less-privileged input process (SEC-014)." Honest reading locked: a control-IN message is a QUERY (joins the SAME cache/inference path, returns TEXT; K-b holds — inbound text can never mint an action) — NOT a shell, NOT command execution, NOT the "operates a workspace" arc (Beyond-Phase-7). The 6-item HARD checklist: (1) hardened/fuzzed inbound parser (fragment-reject) · (2) auth + HMAC-SHA256 (net-new bare-metal, constant-time verify) + nonce/sequence replay (floor PERSISTS across reboot) · (3) rate-limit backed by SCHEDULING (input process pinned off the PA core) · (4) real QUERY SHIELD closing SEC-039 for queries (a query threat model — injection/jailbreak — NOT the action keyword blocklist, which false-positives + has zero injection coverage) · (5) a less-privileged SEC-014 input process (a SUBTRACTION from the PB-spawn cap flow — RX buffer pages + a doorbell + one shmem ring, **NOT BAR0**) · (6) the I211 **RX** bring-up (VIRGIN surface — RX code exists but is gated-NULL `main_x86.c:3339`, never run; the guarded init also OMITS `RXDCTL.ENABLE`, the TX-B2 analog). Architecture: I211 RX → the isolated input process → parser → HMAC/replay → rate-limit → a validated query → PA → real SHIELD → cache/inference (retrieval preamble = prior-session recall) → response over the I211 TX to the install-provisioned console address only → two-way console. M0 (the RX-feasibility spike, box — gate the arc) → M1 (the host-fuzzable security core: parser+HMAC+replay+rate-limit + a fuzz harness, host+CI — the BIGGEST CI win of the phase) → M2 (box: the full RX driver + the SEC-014 process) → M3 (query through PA + close SEC-039 + response + two-way UI) → M4 (a `/security-review` pass + a proven emergency-disable) → the hard-gated `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN` default-0→ON flip ("mostly gated" is a NAMED, FORBIDDEN failure mode). **Pre-mortem-hardened 2026-07-13 (a 6-dimension adversarial-review WORKFLOW, 24 verified findings folded — the heaviest review of the phase):** the DIRECTION held but 9 HIGH defects were corrected — the SEC-014 containment claim was FALSE as first written (a BAR0-holding parser is a bus-master DMA engine with IOMMU-off = root-equivalent → PA keeps BAR0, the process gets RX pages + a doorbell); a cross-reboot / parser-crash replay hole (persistent key + reset-to-0 window → the sequence floor persists + the window is PA-side); the M0 spike would receive NOTHING (missing `RXDCTL.ENABLE`); the action keyword-blocklist is a poor query filter (→ a query threat model); the response LEAKS recalled memory (→ unicast to the console addr only); inbound Q&A is never WRITTEN to episodic (→ the done-when needs the control-IN write, not a synthetic fact); the 6-3 trust seam is LATENT (no v1 TRUST_REQUEST action); + sender-side key provisioning. 15 open questions (O-Q1–O-Q15) for the strategist. Honest ceiling: a bounded, authenticated, rate-limited, SHIELD-scored two-way CONVERSATION on a small model — interactivity behind a hard security gate; "not yet" is the correct default until "provably safe." **FLIP EVIDENCE (M4e, 2026-07-21, boot_id=30, supervised bare metal):** deployed image md5 `ba93fe4ecf8b098e1ccbc513bdbb6d76` verified byte-identical to a rebuild from committed source; V-LOAD **15/15** sustained at human pace (`acc=55` in one boot vs the old 8-frame ceiling); a 24-frame non-routing flood limited at exactly **CAP=8 with 16 dropped**, then **3/3 recovery**; durable `[CTRL-IN-STATS] acc=55 drop=16 (parse=0 rl=16 auth=0 replay=0) bp=0 down=0`; JACT **49 EXECUTED + 21 BLOCKED** with all **25 raw-query probes ZERO** (a refusal records the reason-class LABEL only); `err=0` at `q=175,600`. Rollback = the retained `sel4test-driver-image-x86_64-pc99.bak-pre65flip` md5 `379f6bdb2acfa0c685a710f794723bad` (on the ESP and `~/jarvis_63_image.bak-pre65flip`) and/or dd-zeroing the JKEY slot — both proven; Ubuntu keeps `BootOrder[0]`.
- **Phase 6 Goal 6-6 (Query Routing ≥95%) — PLAN-FIRST:** `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-6_ROUTING.md` — authored 2026-07-22 (post-6-5 next goal), NO code/flag/wire change. **REFRAMES the canon** (`ROADMAP.md:93` device/network/filesystem/user specialists — Phase-1 Python only, ZERO on the box, nothing to route on a headless appliance; RETIRED) to an honest **control-IN query router**: on each QS_ALLOW control-IN query, `route_classify` → SYSTEM-FACTS (answer from a host-whitelisted set of human-meaningful box-state fields) / INFERENCE (Gemma, today's behavior) / DECLINE (status-shaped query with no source → canned "I don't track that", NEVER a fabricated inference) at **≥95% on a KEYWORD-BLIND held-out suite** (host/CI — the biggest CI win of the phase). SYSTEM-FACTS uses the SAME signed+audited+counted+episodic-written exit as INFERENCE (no parallel reply path — would reopen the M4b spoofing hole); REFUSE stays `query_shield`'s upstream verdict (excluded from the routing number); CACHE-on-control-IN deferred. Gated `JARVIS_ROUTING` default-0 → box-proven flip; telemetry v12 rides the EXHAUSTED `TLM_F_CONTROL_IN` flag as added fields (no new flag bit). **The embedding model (train off-box on the RTX 2070, run on the box CPU) is scoped as a SEPARATE next arc (Phase C, §8) — NOT 6-6's ≥95% metric** (PA holds no model/compute so it must run in PB; its honest home is semantic matching on an INDEPENDENT held-out set with a real contrastively-trained decoder-arch embedder). Grounded by a 5-lens research pass + a 4-lens adversarial pre-mortem. 6-6 hard-depends on control-IN (default-ON).
- **Phase 6 Goal 6-7 (7-Day Supervised Exit) — PLAN-FIRST:** `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-7_SOAK.md` — authored 2026-07-24 (the LAST Phase-6 goal), a RUN plan (readiness + observability + measurement + runbook), NO code/flag/wire change unless readiness surfaces a gap. **The done-when IS the JACT action-audit trail** (`ROADMAP.md:99` — "no Level 2+ actions taken without approval"), met STRUCTURALLY: the deployed allowlist (`action_allowlist.c`) holds **exactly FOUR entries**, all bounded L0–1 — RESTART_PB/WAKE_CONSULT=TRUST_NOTIFY, NOTIFY_ANOMALY/STATUS_DIGEST=TRUST_AUTO; TRUST_REQUEST/REQUIRE have no deployed entry. (**Enumeration corrected 2026-07-26:** this previously listed "control-IN action=5=TRUST_NOTIFY" as a fifth allowlist member. It is NOT one — `ACTION_CONTROL_IN_QUERY = 5` is an **AUDIT TAG** with no allowlist entry and therefore **no trust level at all** (`action_allowlist.h`: deliberately not in the table, so K-b holds and it is never LLM-selectable); it exists only so the JACT read-back distinguishes control-IN records from self-heal/monitor/wake 1..4. The structural conclusion is UNCHANGED — nothing deployed sits above L1 — but this done-when rests on enumerating that table, so an inaccurate enumeration weakened the very claim it was cited to support.) So there is nothing to approve — supervision-for-approval has NO OBJECT, named as an honest structural strength. Evidence ranked: PRIMARY = the JACT store (@21,120,000, 4096 CIRCULAR → OFF-BOX SNAPSHOT at EVERY check-in via `dd | parse_action_audit.py`, guards a degraded-week wrap); STABILITY/TOTALS = captured v12 telemetry (per-boot cumulative counters + monotonic q_errors survive a capture gap). Capture = **dumpcap `udp port 51000` on a DEDICATED always-on host** (the receiver's :51000 bind is Hyper-V-excluded; telemetry is BROADCAST so WiFi/the Pi receives it) → a SINGLE ~200 MB file, ≥1h-no-gap proven in readiness. **PASS = 7 days CUMULATIVE uptime** stitched across bounded audited reboots (stores persist), with a **48h checkpoint gate** before committing the week (boot_id=17's 28.7h is the prior envelope — 7 days is ~6× it). **Two honesty corrections folded:** the **<5% FP metric is statistically vacuous on a healthy box** (informs N≈3–6, mostly B4 uptime digests, so "<5% of ~5" is zero-tolerance) → report raw **K/N, PASS=K==0**, cite the 6-3 anti-spam gates as the mechanism proof, do NOT claim "<5% achieved"; and the whole stack runs OUTSIDE seL4's verified X64 set (Fastpath+XSAVE+SMP) → an EMPIRICAL, not verified, stability result. A capture gap is RECOVERABLE (JACT + cumulative totals survive); only a box reboot/power-loss restarts the clock. Deliverable = this doc + a 6-7 FINAL REPORT after the run; if readiness is clean, 6-7 is a RUN, not a build. Phase C (the embedding arc) follows the graduation. Grounded by a 4-lens adversarial pre-mortem.
- **Thinking Mode — research + verdict (CLOSED):** `phase6/docs/THINKING_MODE_RESEARCH.md` — **`JARVIS_THINKING` stays 0 for THIS model and THIS workload, on convergent evidence** (our pre-registered A/B, the workload, and published evaluation of the family at this size). **NOT a claim that thinking modes don't work.** Also the authoritative record of the **bench-off harness finding** (§7) — the 8.40/10 was raw-completion, and the two traps any re-bench must avoid.
- **Blind-set label key (front-load-only round):** `models/quality_results_v2/BLIND_SET_KEY.md` — which system produced which text in `BLIND_SET.md`. **TWO ARMS PROVEN byte-exact (the baselines, which carried over unchanged), TWO INFERRED from label continuity (the front-loaded arms) — the file states which per row; do not quote the table without that distinction.** A SEPARATE file so `BLIND_SET.md` stays blind and re-judgeable. **The front-load-only PER-MODEL outputs do NOT survive** (a `${PREFIX:+.frontload}` filename collision overwrote them; the responses survive in `BLIND_SET.md`, the per-answer tok/s and harness headers do not). Carries the scoring caveat: that round's rubric gained a fifth rule, so **within-run deltas stand and cross-run absolutes do not.** **The generator's own keys for ALL THREE bench rounds are committed beside it** (`blind_key_rebench_4model.json` / `_frontload_twoclause.json` / `_frontload_only.json` — recovered from a reapable temp scratchpad, so the re-bench "incumbent is third" ranking now has committed provenance). **TRAP: `SYS-n` labels are fixed WITHIN a blind set but NOT ACROSS rounds** — in the re-bench `SYS-2` is E4B and `SYS-4` is E2B-recommended-sampler, and because two of the four labels coincidentally DO agree with the front-loading rounds, a spot-check reads as consistent and then misattributes E4B's answers to E2B. Read the key for the round you are looking at.
- **Model bench-off, re-run on a FIXED harness (2026-07-27):** `phase6/docs/MODEL_BENCH_2026-07.md` — **the harness fix alone CHANGED THE RANKING.** Llama 3.1 8B scored 5.06/10 and was DISQUALIFIED in 2026-04; templated correctly it ranks **first (7.7)**. Gemma 4 E4B **7.5** (tied). **The incumbent Gemma 4 E2B is THIRD (6.1) on its own workload.** The Gemma-card sampler (t=1.0/top_p .95/top_k 64) measured **worse** than greedy (5.8 vs 6.1) — **no free win, keep greedy.** NOTHING SWAPPED, nothing deployed — evidence only, the decision is the operator's. Two caveats that bound it: the ranking is driven by behaviour under the **250-token cap** (Llama answers first, the Gemmas preamble first and get cut — raise the cap and it could invert), and the quality figures are **llama.cpp's, not our engine's**. **FRONT-LOAD TEST 2026-07-28 (§8 of that doc) — the mechanism is REAL but the fix is NET-HARMFUL, and the model question STANDS.** One deployable 13-token instruction (*"Answer the question directly in your first sentence, then elaborate."*, placed in the g3-preamble slot) took the incumbent's substance-delivery from **1/5 to 5/5** on the open-ended questions — the structural hypothesis is confirmed as a MECHANISM — but the **quality score FELL** (subject 7.0→6.4) and the **control COLLAPSED** (Llama 8.0→4.0), because the *"then elaborate"* clause overrode *"in one line"* and drove length violations to **7 of 12** on the control. **Pre-registered verdict applied: "E2B falls → instructions consume prompt budget and can distort."** The two clauses did opposite things (open-unanswered 3.0→1.0 vs length violations 0.6→3.2), so **an instruction without *"then elaborate"* is UNTESTED and is the obvious next experiment — that is a hypothesis, not a claim it would work.** **A cap raise is separately RULED OUT by arithmetic:** max answer 1313 B vs the 1426 B ceiling is **23 tokens (9%)**, while reaching Gemma's substance needs ~1.37× the ceiling — that needs multi-frame (#14). **Also fixed there: a real harness bug that corrupted content in BOTH bench runs** — the chrome-strip ran on every line, eating `/**`→`**`, `//`, markdown bullets and ALL code indentation. Symmetric across arms so the rankings stand, but **Q12 (the C-function question) cannot be scored from the committed outputs**; the harness is now correct.
- **Model options — what a bigger model would cost:** `phase6/docs/MODEL_OPTIONS_2026-07.md` — RESEARCH, no decision taken. Bandwidth (~15.8 GB/s), not RAM, is the binding constraint, so a bigger model costs speed **linearly**; low-bit (Bonsai-class) is the only lever that breaks that trade, and it needs an AVX2 `qdot` + an H2-whitelist edit. **Every tok/s in it except 5.46 is arithmetic, not measurement — the doc says so and that labelling must survive any edit.**
- **Phase C (Small Embedding Model / semantic matching) — PLAN-FIRST:** `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_C_EMBEDDER.md` — authored 2026-07-24 (the neural arc AFTER the 6-7 graduation), NO code/flag/wire change. Adds a small embedding model (runs on the box CPU in PB; trained/tuned off-box on the RTX 2070 ONLY if a lane misses) for SEMANTIC MATCHING. **First lane = semantic RECALL** (closes 6-5's documented exact-repeat-only limit — "state a fact, then ask a DIFFERENT question"); routing + semantic cache DEFERRED (measured-miss-gated). Locked direction from a 4-lens research pass: (a) a **DECODER-style embedder** (reuses the box's Gemma-arch load/forward/tokenizer/kernels — the pooling tap already exists at `llama_quant.c:1801`, post-final-norm/pre-LM-head; a BERT encoder is REJECTED as a net-new second engine), lead candidate **EmbeddingGemma-300M** (Gemma-arch GGUF, Matryoshka→128); (b) **off-the-shelf FIRST** — a 2070 contrastive fine-tune is a LATER contingency scoped ONLY to a measured routing miss (weak zero-shot intent), never pre-committed; (d) runs in PB with a `MSG_EMBED` IPC path, cosine == dot over the existing `dot_f32_avx2`, NO ANN at this scale; (e) **the PARITY HARNESS is the silent-failure guard** — the C engine must reproduce the Python/llama.cpp golden vectors to ~1e-3 (pooling type + prefix + L2-norm replicated exactly), gating C/M1; (f) **the GATE is OUR data, not MTEB** (narrow domain; the 6-6 held-out discipline). Milestones: **C/M0 (OFF-BOX Python — gates the whole arc)** prototypes EmbeddingGemma-300M vs the bge-small-en-v1.5 33M baseline on our labeled data (routing_suite.h paraphrase clusters + control-IN seeds + a hand-authored "state-a-fact/ask-different" recall set), scoring nearest-centroid held-out accuracy (target ≥~90%) + positive/negative separation, and SAVES the golden vectors for C/M1 parity — the score picks the model AND decides off-the-shelf-vs-fine-tune, NO box code → C/M1 (box, gated `JARVIS_EMBED` default-0, pre-mortem first: embed-mode forward + co-resident 2nd GGUF + `MSG_EMBED` + the parity harness) → C/M2 (the recall lane — cosine-topk over per-record vectors in the dedicated control-IN store @21,140,000, replacing `g3_select_exact_only`, P6/P7 preamble hygiene preserved) → C/M3 (telemetry + console + the `JARVIS_EMBED` flip) → C/M4+ (routing + semantic cache, measured-miss-gated). Honest ceiling: **meaning-based recall of the box's OWN prior answers, NOT "understands"/"remembers your conversation" as reasoning.** **C/M0 DONE (off-box, RTX 2070, 2026-07-24) — GATE = DO NOT PROCEED to C/M1 on an off-the-shelf model** (`phase3/scripts/embed/`): scored bge-small/bge-large/e5-large/EmbeddingGemma-300M on our own labeled data; **no off-the-shelf model clears the recall bar** (~≥90% top-1 WITH clean separation) on our narrow domain — best is bge-large **81% top-1 / 88% top-3** (but a net-new BERT engine + still no clean separation), the strong-reuse EmbeddingGemma-300M is among the WEAKEST (**50% top-1**), and every model is strongly anisotropic (unrelated-pair cosine floor ~0.5–0.7 → overlap = false-recall risk). **The plan's prediction INVERTED:** RECALL is the hard lane (not routing — intent clustering is 83–93% off-the-shelf, though still below the 6-6 keyword router's 95.89%). **Recommended next step: a 2070 CONTRASTIVE FINE-TUNE of a Gemma-arch embedder** (fixes the measured recall miss + anisotropy via hard negatives, preserves the near-zero-box-engine reuse). Golden-vector save (§4) DEFERRED — it is C/M1 setup for the chosen model, and the gate reopened the model choice before box work. Methodology note: fixing asym→symmetric query-to-query + a topically-distinct corpus moved EmbeddingGemma 16.7%→50% (a misuse ≠ a result); full write-up in the goal doc's C/M0 FINDINGS. **C/M0.5 DONE (off-box, 2026-07-24) — the C/M0 gate was BASE-LIMITED and is SUPERSEDED: WINNER = `Qwen/Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B`, which CLEARS THE RECALL BAR OFF-THE-SHELF** (97.2% recall@1 / 100% top-3 / CLEAN separation with a cheap frozen mean-projection, on an enlarged N=36 distinct set — the ONLY model with a positive margin + clean separation; next best gte-large 80.6/97.2, EmbeddingGemma only 30.6). C/M0's "off-the-shelf fails" was an artifact of testing the WRONG bases (raw EmbeddingGemma — Gemma-arch but not a strong contrastive embedder — + BERT encoders), NOT domain-intractability; a contrastively-trained DECODER embedder wins on accuracy AND separation at partial box-reuse cost (the Qwen3 path exists from the bench-off). **PLAN-CHANGING FINDING (surfaced, not buried): fine-tuning is NOT needed for the recall lane** — the C/M0.5 brief locked "we ARE fine-tuning" on C/M0's superseded numbers, but the data falsifies that premise, so the faithful path (the doc's own §2b "off-the-shelf FIRST; fine-tune only on a MEASURED miss") is: adopt off-the-shelf Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B, go straight to **C/M1 (box engine + parity)**, and DEMOTE the 2070 fine-tune (C/M1a) to a measured-miss contingency after C/M2. Golden vectors SAVED for the winner (`phase3/scripts/embed/golden_vectors.npz` + `golden_meta.json`, sym:none + frozen mu). C/M0.5 FINDINGS in the goal doc. **C/M1a FOUNDATION DONE (off-box, 2026-07-24; the C-engine port is the remaining phase) — the HOST-parity two-golden methodology** (`cm1a_golden.py`): `gguf_golden.npz` (official Qwen GGUF at Q8_0 via llama-cpp-python, last-pool+L2 — the TIGHT engine-vs-engine ~1e-4 target, its token-ids the token-parity reference incl. EOS 151643) + `golden_vectors.npz` (ST F32 sym:none + frozen mu — the loose target); **MEASURED quant floor** = GGUF-Q8_0-vs-ST-F32 cosine 0.99915–0.99965 → honest F32 tolerance **1.27e-3** (not a guessed 1e-3; the 0.999+ cross-engine cosine also confirms the last-pool + EOS config). 3 parity gates in `golden_meta.json` (token-parity GREEN → engine-vs-engine ≤1e-4 → F32 ≤1.27e-3). REMAINING C-engine port (all `#if JARVIS_EMBED`-gated): a merge-RANK BPE path for qwen in `tokenizer.c` (deployed = score-priority SentencePiece, WRONG for qwen; merges already loaded in `gguf_vocab.c`), RoPE-NEOX for qwen3, a gated embed-mode forward (last-pool @`llama_quant.c:1801` → skip LM head → L2 → mean-project → L2), a host harness (`-DJARVIS_EMBED=1 -mavx2 -mfma`) + CI. Milestone naming reconciled: **C/M1a = HOST parity (this), C/M1b = BOX plumbing, the 2070 fine-tune = a measured-miss CONTINGENCY** (not pre-committed — off-the-shelf Qwen3 clears the bar). **C/M1a STAGE 1 (token parity) DONE 2026-07-24 — 15/15 golden probes byte-EXACT with ZERO tokenizer.c changes** (`phase3/src/ai/embed_tokenize_probe.c` host harness gated `JARVIS_EMBED` + `phase3/scripts/embed/cm1a_token_parity.py` the diff gate): the merge-rank scaffold (`gguf_vocab.c` merges→`score=-merge_index` + the GPT-2 Ġ marker + the harness EOS-151643 append) already reproduces the qwen tokenization, INCLUDING the contraction cases the pre-mortem predicted would break ("what's"→[what,'s], "doesn't"→[doesn,'t]); the **predicted GPT-2 pre-split gap did NOT materialize on the gate**. A 33-string realistic robustness stress (`cm1a_stress_strings.txt`) = **32/33**, the ONLY divergence being consecutive/double spaces (a whitespace pre-tokenization nuance needing the full pre-split infra — DEFERRED as a scoped C/M2-hardening step, not rushed into Stage 1 where it'd risk regressing the passing cases; "measure first, fix only what diverges"). Deployed tokenizer byte-identical at EMBED=0 by construction (zero tokenizer.c lines added). No CI step (model-gated harness — the model-gated-SKIP precedent). **C/M1a STAGE 2 (VECTOR PARITY) DONE 2026-07-25 → C/M1a HOST PARITY COMPLETE; the C-engine port is PROVEN and box work (C/M1b) is unblocked.** The gated qwen NEOX-RoPE override + `qmodel_embed_last` (last-pool at the `:1801` tap → L2) reproduce the reference embeddings **to float32 machine epsilon**: GATE 2′ worst `1−cos = 0.00e+00` on 15/15, GATE 3 worst 3.69e-4 vs the 1.268e-3 measured floor. **The headline is that the GATE was wrong, not the port** — the original ≤1e-4 engine-vs-engine GATE 2 is MIS-SPECIFIED BY CONSTRUCTION (llama.cpp quantizes ACTIVATIONS; JARVIS keeps them F32), so it was **REPAIRED into GATE 2′ (same-precision, on a lossless BF16→F32 GGUF = zero weight delta), not deleted** — deleting it would have left GATE 3's ~3.4× headroom band for a subtle port bug to hide in. `llama_load.c` + `llama_quant.c` proven EMBED=0 object-identical AND preprocessed-source-identical. Full numbers + the two defects found along the way (a >2 GB `ftell` overflow on Windows; md5-of-`.o` being an invalid identity test under COFF build timestamps) are in the goal doc's C/M1a Stage 2 FINDINGS + the `JARVIS_EMBED` flag row.
- **Embed Vector-Parity Harness (Phase C / C/M1a Stage 2, HOST-ONLY, model-gated-local):** `phase3/src/ai/embed_vector_probe.c` — loads a Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B GGUF through the DEPLOYED engine (`qmodel_load` → `gguf_vocab`/`tokenizer` → `qmodel_embed_last`), encodes the 15 golden probes, appends EOS 151643, and writes 15×1024 raw LE float32 vectors for the comparator. MEASURE-FIRST: prints the loaded arch/`rope_neox`/`embed_scale`/`use_gelu`/`rope_theta` and **hard-FAILs if a qwen arch loaded with `rope_neox=0`** (the silent-parity breaker). Self-guards `#ifndef JARVIS_EMBED → 0` (inert stub in a default build); takes the model path as `argv[1]`, so ONE binary serves both the Q8_0 and F32 runs. Uses 64-bit file offsets (`_fseeki64` on Windows — `long` is 32-bit there, and an F32 GGUF of this model is 2.38 GB, so plain `ftell` overflows and the read fails before any inference; verified behavior-neutral — Q8_0 output md5-identical before/after). **NEVER in the deployed build or CI.** Companion `phase3/src/ai/embed_host_fmemopen.h` — a `_WIN32`-only `fmemopen` PROTOTYPE force-included (`-include`) into every host TU so `gguf_parser.c` compiles under the MSVC CRT (which lacks POSIX `fmemopen`); the DEFINITION lives in the harness (DELETE_ON_CLOSE temp-file backed, self-cleaning), keeping `<windows.h>` and its `min`/`max`/`ERROR` macros out of the engine sources. Host-harness support ONLY — never compiled into the seL4 build or CI.
- **Embed Vector-Parity GATE (Phase C / C/M1a Stage 2):** `phase3/scripts/embed/cm1a_vector_parity.py` — the committed comparator. Takes the C Q8_0 vectors plus `--c-f32` (the matched-precision run) and prints ALL 15 per-probe cosines/gaps/max-abs BEFORE any verdict: **(a) GATE 2′** C-F32 vs the ST F32 golden = THE port-correctness gate, on **pre-registered bands** (≤1e-5 noise / ≤1e-4 arithmetic-order / >1e-4 = real discrepancy — fixed in advance so a verdict cannot be re-based after seeing numbers); **(b)** C-F32 vs C-Q8_0 = our OWN weight-quant delta, sanity-checked against llama.cpp's measured 8.45e-4; **(c)** C-Q8_0 vs the llama.cpp Q8_0 golden = the cross-engine **DIAGNOSTIC** (the original GATE 2 — reported with its "would have said FAIL" verdict stated honestly, never silently passed); plus **GATE 3** at the measured floor. Exits 0 only if GATE 2′ AND GATE 3 pass; exits 2 (INCOMPLETE) if `--c-f32` is omitted, so the port gate can never be skipped by accident. Model-gated-local (no CI step).
- **Embed Region staleness predicate (Phase C / C/M1b-3, host):** `phase3/src/ipc/embed_region.c/h` — the PA↔PB embedding transport's control header + `embed_result_usable()`. **A 1024-float vector does NOT travel through the response ring** (4096 B = 18 chunks into a 15-slot, 240 B-payload ring — impossible regardless of how well the chunk loop handles a full ring), so it travels through a DEDICATED 2-page region and the ring carries only a small typed request/completion. That leaves exactly ONE place a subtle bug hides — deciding whether the bytes in the page answer the request you just made — which is pure logic, so it is extracted and host-tested per the unbroken precedent (`km2b_miss`/`km2b_trigger`/`wake`/`control_floor`/`control_console`; `main_x86.c`/`inference_server.c` are seL4-only and NEVER host-compiled). **THE CASE IT EXISTS FOR: a STALE-but-READY page** — if PA accepts a result whose `seq` is the PREVIOUS request's, it silently returns the previous question's embedding, **a perfectly well-formed unit vector of the wrong text that a cosine check CANNOT detect**. **`seq` is compared by EQUALITY, never ordering** — at the uint32 wrap the newest seq is numerically the SMALLEST, so any `>=`/`>` inverts exactly once every 2³² requests. `len` is checked explicitly too, because a vector missing its last few floats still scores cosine ≈1.0. Publication is release/acquire (the SCTX/mailbox discipline): producer writes the vector then RELEASE-stores `ready` LAST; consumer ACQUIRE-loads `ready` FIRST. **`_Static_assert(EMBED_VEC_BYTES == 4096)`** pins that 1024 floats × 4 B is exactly one page with zero spare — the dimension is NOT permanent (the Phase C plan contemplates Matryoshka truncation to 128), so a future change must BREAK THE BUILD rather than silently corrupt a page boundary. `test_embed_region.c` **15 PASS** (8 required cases + the wrap quartet + a NULL guard + a TEETH case pinning that `seq` alone decides usability) → CI "Phase C: C/M1b-3 embed region staleness (C)". **Teeth-proven by mutation:** deleting the `seq` check fails 4 tests (T3/T7c/T7d/TEETH). Transport wiring itself (`MSG_EMBED`) is T2 proper — this module is logic only.
- **Phase 6 Goal 6-5 FINAL REPORT:** `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-5_FINAL_REPORT.md` — the standalone 6-5 final report (what shipped per checklist item, the module/CI inventory, the flip + validation evidence, and the honest limitations carried forward).
- **Fault-EP Validity Predicate (Phase 6 K/M4 pre-flip, host):** `phase3/src/ai/km2b_fault.c/h` — the `pa_poll_fault` label-gate **defense-in-depth** hardening ahead of the deploy-live `JARVIS_ACTIONS` flip (§10 carry-forward #2). `km2b_fault_is_genuine(label,badge,ip,n_workers,text_lo,text_hi)` — a fault-EP `seL4_NBRecv` result is GENUINE (not an empty-EP phantom) iff **label ∈ 1..15 AND badge ∈ 0..`g_pb_workers_started`**; the badge is a SEPARATE stale-register class the STEP-3 label-only gate was blind to, so requiring BOTH is multiplicatively less likely to coincide. `km2b_fault_ip_plausible` is **ADVISORY only** (logs `[FAULT-IP-WARN]`, NEVER a veto — a genuine jump-to-garbage VMFault legitimately faults outside `.text` yet MUST still restart); it returns 0 only for a VMFault (label 5) whose ip is outside PB's `.text` window `[lo,hi)`, and degrades to always-plausible when the window is unknown. The window is stashed once at spawn (`stash_pb_text_window`, the PF_X PT_LOAD union; ET_EXEC ⇒ vaddr==runtime addr). Wired into `pa_poll_fault` (all `#if JARVIS_ACTIONS`) after the label gate + before the `[FAULT]` print — a rejected phantom logs `[FAULT-REJECT]` + `break`s, never reaching `pa_restart_pb`; a genuine fault is NEVER rejected (host X3 + the box gate: 0 false `[FAULT-REJECT]` across the induced-fault probes). `test_km2b_fault.c` **21/21** (incl. the teeth-proven X3 advisory-≠-verdict guard — a mutant folding the advisory into the verdict fails X3 alone) → CI "Phase 6: K/M4 fault validity predicate (C)". Host-pure (no seL4 dep, mirrors `km2b_miss.h`); OFF (`JARVIS_ACTIONS=0`) byte-identical by construction (whole wiring + the stash gated). Called only under `#if JARVIS_ACTIONS` (default 0).
- **Hang/Wedge Miss-Counter (Phase 6 K/M2c, host):** `phase3/src/ai/km2b_miss.c/h` — the shared consecutive-PB-contact-miss state machine that catches PB **alive-but-WEDGED** (no fault fires, the fault EP stays silent — the failure the STEP-3 crash lane can't see). `km2b_miss_on_pb_timeout(lane)` advances, `km2b_miss_on_pb_ack` resets to 0 (on a GENUINE typed dequeue only, never a bare `seL4_Wait`), `km2b_miss_tripped(threshold)` = `count >= threshold`. The counter moves ONLY on explicit calls — no "absence looks like an event" (the STEP-3 phantom class). Wired at the 3 PB-contact timeout/success sites in `main_x86.c` (all `#if JARVIS_ACTIONS`); the cache lane + `cg_served_now` call NEITHER (D4 neutrality); the single `next_query` trigger funnels `pa_restart_pb("hang", …)` at threshold 3. `test_km2b_miss.c` **22/22** (+T11 NULL-arg / T12 threshold-0) → CI "Phase 6: K/M2c hang miss-counter (C)". Box-proven: 5/5 hang cycles `miss_count=3 fault_EP=silent -> TRIPPED -> coherent recovery`; the JACT trail (`respawn hang lane=2 miss=3`) reads back off-box. Detects deschedule-able wedges (blocked-on-IPC / yield-spin). A hard same-core busy-loop is **CONFIRMED NOT detectable** (K/M4 box experiment 2026-07-07, **Outcome B**): a non-yielding PB-main `for(;;){}` STARVES PA on the shared core 0 (PA's production yield-poll hands the core to PB), so PA emits 0 detection lines while a soft-yield hang was detected 5/5 in the same run — only deschedule-able wedges + crashes (fault deschedules PB) are caught. Mitigation = a dedicated PA core (K/M4 carry-forward #3), or accept the documented limitation (hard non-yielding loop → power-cycle, a narrow class). **D1/D2 follow-up (next commit):** the inference dispatch guard decoupled from `#if JARVIS_CACHE_GROWTH`; the hang-trip polls the fault EP first so a crash-during-HB/shield-wait audits as "fault" not "hang".
- **Control-IN Security Core (Phase 6 6-5/M1, host-pure — NOW LIVE IN THE DEPLOYED IMAGE since the 6-5 flip 2026-07-21; the "DEPLOY-INERT" status below is M1-era history):** `phase3/src/crypto/` (`sha256.c/h` = copied public-domain Brad-Conte SHA-256, 3 NIST KATs; `hmac_sha256.c/h` = RFC 2104 + a **constant-time** `hmac_sha256_verify` — `volatile` OR-accumulator over all 32 tag bytes, NO early-out, deliberately NOT the `shmem_ipc.c` `!=` compare; `{sha256,hmac}_vectors.h` = committed differential-fuzz vectors) + `phase3/src/net/` (`control_msg.h` = the authoritative 36-B-header/≤240-B wire format, big-endian, **canonical HMAC span = payload[0..len−32)**; `control_parser.c/h` = hardened copy-free Eth/IPv4/UDP+JCTL parse — **fragment-reject (MF|offset) before any UDP/HMAC work**, exact length math, `IP_MIN_HDR`=20 guard; `control_replay.c/h` = PA-side seq-floor + epoch + 128-nonce ring, ordered epoch→seq→nonce truth table, persistence contract; `control_ratelimit.c/h` = uint32-wrap-safe milli-token bucket (millisecond-driven by the CALLER — feeding it a per-frame counter starves the channel at exactly CAP; see the M4e finding); `control_verify.c/h` = the orchestrator, order parse→ratelimit→HMAC→replay so **`control_replay_check` MUTATES state ONLY after a valid HMAC** — an unauth frame can never walk the floor / fill the ring; `fuzz_control_in.c` = 300K-iter ASan/UBSan) + `phase3/scripts/gen_control_vectors.py`. Built by a 5-phase Workflow (crypto leaves verified vs NIST/RFC vectors BEFORE dependents) + an adversarial-review agent that caught a **`len==14` 1-byte parser OOB read** (`ip[0]` with only `len≥14`) → fixed + the fuzz hardened to **exact-len heap buffers** (ASan now redzones any read-past-`len`; teeth-proven vs a guard-removed mutant → `heap-buffer-overflow READ`). **Per-module CI counts (re-measured 2026-07-21, all green):** SHA-256 = 3 NIST KATs · HMAC-SHA256 = RFC 4231 cases 1/2/4/6 + the constant-time verify sweep · `control_parser` **43** · `control_replay` **23** · `control_ratelimit` **13** (was 9; +4 at the M4e clock fix) · `control_verify` **22** · `fuzz_control_in` **300,020** iters ASan/UBSan. **M1-era status (history): deploy-inert — nothing linked into the box.** M2a wired it into the build behind `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`; the SEC-014 less-privileged input process is M2b; the query SHIELD (SEC-039-for-queries) is M3. **CURRENT: all of this is LIVE in the deployed image** (`JARVIS_CONTROL_IN` default-ON since the 6-5 flip 2026-07-21) — every inbound frame is parsed, rate-limited, HMAC-verified and replay-checked by this code on real hardware. Doc: `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-5_CONTROL_IN.md` §9.
- **Control-IN RX Data Path (Phase 6 6-5/M2a, box — `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN` is DEFAULT-ON since 2026-07-21, so this RX path is LIVE; "default-0 / DEPLOY-INERT" below is M2a-era history):** the RX-productization half of the goal-doc M2 — real I211 RX ring → the M1 `control_verify` (running in PA) → LOG the validated query ONLY (the **M3 BOUNDARY**: no routing to inference; the route + query SHIELD is M3). `nic_i211.c` gains gated step-6 RX programming (SRRCTL-legacy + `RXDCTL.ENABLE` the M0 silent-RX fix + RDT-armed-last; `#else` = verbatim HEAD `RDT=255` for obj-identity — nic_i211.c doesn't include jarvis_debug.h so the gate rides a build-script `-DJARVIS_CONTROL_IN=1`). `main_x86.c` (all `#if JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`): the [NET] block allocs the RX ring + 256 buffers and arms RDT only if all 256 wired → `g_net.nic`; boot reads the HMAC key FAIL-CLOSED from the NVMe JKEY slot (`control_key.h` @ LBA 21,130,000; key lives in PA, never the future input process, never BAR0) + inits `control_replay`/`control_ratelimit` (fixed `CONTROL_TEST_EPOCH`, per-boot floor 0 — M3 = real epoch + persisted floor); the workload-loop poll (`i211_nic_recv`→`control_verify`) LOGs a sanitized ACCEPT (durable, bounded to 8) / silent DROP counters; `[CTRL-IN-STATS]` durable at the [STATS] cadence. `control_verify` gains additive `seq`/`boot_epoch` (set on CV_ACCEPT only; M1 CI still green). `build_jarvis_x86.sh` GATES the crypto/net source copy + CMake inject + the `-D` + include dirs on the header flag being 1, with a **symmetric teardown `else`** (an adversarial-review HIGH: the persistent `-D` overrode the header's `#ifndef 0` → a silent "OFF-stays-ON" after an ON rebuild; the teardown strips the injected state). **Full box gate PASSED 2026-07-15:** OFF-identity (main.c.obj+nic_i211.c.obj byte-identical, proven 3× incl. post-teardown) + KVM probe (`JARVIS_CONTROL_IN_PROBE`: key-loaded → synthetic frame ACCEPT + tamper DROP_AUTH) + **bare-metal signed frames** (boot 22 unicast + boot 23 broadcast, both accepted: `[CTRL-IN] ACCEPT seq=1/2/3` + `[CTRL-IN-STATS] acc=3 (auth=1 replay=1)`, err=0, LAN/telemetry broadcast parse-dropped on wrong port). A host cross-check (scapy frame → C `control_verify` → ACCEPT/DROP_AUTH) proved the wire format before the box. Doc §9; flags `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`/`_PROBE`.
- **Control-IN Input Process (Phase 6 6-5/M2b-1, box — LIVE since the 6-5 flip 2026-07-21; `phase3/src/sel4/input_server.c`, 108 LOC. "default-0 / DEPLOY-INERT" below is M2b-1-era history):** the SEC-014 **isolation split** (Model 2, goal-doc §5) — the untrusted PARSE moves OFF Process A into a NEW least-privileged third seL4 process `jarvis-input` (a sibling CPIO app to `jarvis-inference`). `phase3/src/sel4/input_server.c` (the process main: `seL4_Wait(wake)` → read the PA→input RAW mailbox → `control_parse_frame`/`control_parse_msg` + `control_ratelimit` ONLY → publish the isolated JCTL bytes + a status hint to the input→PA CAND mailbox → `seL4_Signal(ack)`) + `phase3/src/net/control_mailbox.h` (the two 4 KB release/acquire mailboxes, x86-TSO handshake, single-frame-in-flight backpressure). **The input process holds NO timer caps and has no clock, so PA STAMPS `ctrl_raw_mbx_t.fwd_ms` (`jarvis_uptime_ms`) into the raw mailbox alongside the payload — published by the same RELEASE store — and the input process feeds THAT to `control_ratelimit`** (M4e pre-flip fix; M2b-1 shipped a per-frame `tick++` placeholder that refilled 1 milli-token per frame against a 1000 milli-token cost, so the channel accepted exactly 8 frames and then died — bare-metal-confirmed `[CTRL-IN-STATS] acc=8 drop=2 (… rl=2 …)`. Fails CLOSED, no security hole, but it made the standing-conversation claim false). The input process holds **ZERO NIC caps, no HMAC key, no request/response ring, no model caps** — a cap-subtraction from `spawn_inference_process` (it gets only its TCB/CSpace/VSpace/IPC buffer + the 2 mailbox frames + 2 notifications + CPU, `seL4_MaxPrio-1` pinned off PA's core). PA keeps BAR0 + polls the NIC + copies each raw frame into the RAW mailbox; `main_x86.c` (all `#if JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`): `spawn_input_process` (config + map/dup the 2 mailbox frames into PA + input + copy the 2 notif caps + argv the child cslots), `pa_verify_candidate` (PA **RE-PARSES** the candidate ITSELF then HMAC + replay — verify-in-PA, O-Q3c, never trusts a forwarded offset), and the live-poll rewrite (consume-candidate then forward-one-frame); the rate limiter moved off PA INTO the input process. `build_jarvis_x86.sh` creates the `jarvis-input` app + registers it (`add_subdirectory(../jarvis-input)` + extends `MakeCPIO`) under the gate, with a symmetric teardown `else` so an OFF build packs no input ELF. **Box gate PASSED 2026-07-15:** OFF object-identity (`main.c.obj` .text/.rodata/.data + `nm` byte-identical to the pre-M2b-1 baseline; no `jarvis-input` ELF / `crypto`/`net` objects when off) + KVM split-pipeline PROBE (`-smp 6`, CONTROL_IN=1/PROBE=1): `[CTRL-IN-PROBE] accept q=status cand_seq=1` (the round trip PA→input→PA — `cand_seq` PROVES the input process produced the candidate, not a PA-only shortcut) + `tamper=DROP_AUTH`, `[CTRL-IN] input process spawned (SEC-014, parse+ratelimit only)`, 5 workers/NN=6, coherent Gemma, err=0, 0 faults/restarts. No telemetry/console change (on-wire control-IN telemetry is M3). M2b-2 = bare-metal + flood-doesn't-starve-PA + liveness-heartbeat + drop-`RCTL.BAM` hardening. Doc §9; flags `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`/`_PROBE`.
- **Control-IN Input-Process Hardening (Phase 6 6-5/M2b-2, box — LIVE since the 6-5 flip 2026-07-21; "default-0 / DEPLOY-INERT" below is M2b-2-era history):** the SEC-014 **liveness + degrade + flood/SEC-033 + drop-BAM** hardening that **CLOSES goal-doc item-5** (DONE 2026-07-16: KVM-proven + the supervised bare-metal WIRE PROOF, boot_id=24 — `acc=3` / `DROP_REPLAY` / `DROP_AUTH`, the flood rate-limited `rl`→488 with err=0 to q=13,700 / 0 faults / `bp=0` / `down=0`, and `parse=0` = all broadcast hardware-filtered by the dropped BAM; box reverted clean to the 6-3 image). **Liveness:** a new `km2b_miss` DEADLINE-WINDOW lane `KM2B_LANE_INPUT=5` (`km2b_miss.h`) — a wedged input sticks `g_ctrl_inflight=1` so PA stops forwarding (a frame-counted miss would never trip), so the miss advances once per `CTRL_IN_DEADLINE_ITERS`=32 PA-active iterations one in-flight frame stays unanswered (q_total-keyed → the deadline FREEZES during an inference, so a live input starved by worker-5 never false-trips; idle-safe). At `CTRL_IN_MISS_THRESHOLD`=3 → latch `g_ctrl_in_down` + `mon_notify(MON_EV_INPUT_DEAD)` → `[ANOMALY] mon input-dead` + JACT `action=2` + `monitors_fired++` through the K spine (zero new plumbing). DETECT + DEGRADE only (a jarvis-input respawn is M3 — the leaky allocator forbids naive respawn); a dead input = control-IN unavailable until reboot. **`main_x86.c`** (all `#if JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`): the `ctrl_in_liveness_tick(now_iters)` shared helper (poll site passes `q_total`, PROBE passes synthetic counts), the poll-site outer `!g_ctrl_in_down` gate + `km2b_miss_on_pb_ack` on candidate-consume + the deadline tick + the STAMP on forward + the backpressure drain-into-`bp_scratch`+`g_ctrl_bp_drops++` else-branch, the `[CTRL-IN-STATS]` `bp=`/`down=` fields, and PROBE mode 2 (`seL4_TCB_Suspend` the input TCB → 3 ticks → degrade; INSIDE the `g_input_ready` guard). **`monitors.h`/`monitors.c`** (`#if JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`): `MON_EV_INPUT_DEAD` + its keyword-clean `"mon input-dead lane=N miss=N"` snapshot case (T7-pinned via a new `-DJARVIS_CONTROL_IN=1` CI compile). **`nic_i211.c`:** RCTL drops `BAM` (unicast-only RX; RA[0]/AV exact-MAC filter is BAM-independent; TX telemetry unaffected) under `#if JARVIS_CONTROL_IN && !defined(JARVIS_CONTROL_IN_BAM)`; the `#else` = verbatim HEAD (`nic_i211.c.obj` OFF-identical). **`jarvis_debug.h`:** PROBE-mode doc + `#error` cross-guards (CONTROL_IN_PROBE vs other `*_PROBE`; PROBE==2 requires MONITORS && ACTIONS) + the `CONTROL_IN_BAM` escape-hatch note. Pre-mortem-hardened (a 6-lens adversarial-review WORKFLOW — 33 findings, 9 folded) + a 3-lens diff-review (clean). Box gate: **OFF 4-object identity** (`main.c.obj`+`nic_i211.c.obj`+`monitors.c.obj`+`km2b_miss.c.obj` .text/.rodata/.data + `nm` byte-identical) + host green (`test_monitors` host-pure **and** `-DJARVIS_CONTROL_IN=1` both 44 PASS, `test_km2b_miss` 22 PASS) + KVM induced-death PROBE. Option-A scheduling confirmed (input MaxPrio-1 on core `g_num_nodes-1`; Option B = a permanent ~15–20% Gemma hit, fallback only). Doc §9; flags `JARVIS_CONTROL_IN`/`_PROBE`(=2 induced-death)/`_BAM`.
- **Control-IN Query SHIELD (Phase 6 6-5/M3-1, host-pure — NOW LIVE on the deployed box since the 6-5 flip 2026-07-21, scoring every inbound query before PA routes it; "HOST + CI ONLY / DEPLOY-INERT" below is M3-1-era history):** the query-threat gate that closes **SEC-039-for-queries at the LOGIC level** — a control-IN frame that survived M1/M2b auth+replay yields a ≤172 B validated QUERY, and `query_shield.c/h` scores it QS_ALLOW vs QS_REFUSE for four DEFINED abuse classes (KEY_EXTRACTION / EXFIL / JAILBREAK / CONFIG_DISCLOSE) BEFORE PA would route it (the box route + induced-BLOCK proof + response are M3-2). **THE HONESTY CEILING (O-Q4, the crux):** a COARSE abuse-refuser for tractable classes, NOT an injection detector — a local Gemma + no injection detector can't catch novel/obfuscated prompt injection; injection is contained STRUCTURALLY (K-b: inbound text can never mint an action; tagged-untrusted → no cache-growth/retrieval store contamination; answer-to-console-address-only → no exfil). The ONLY honest claim: *"refuses a defined abuse class + audits it; general injection contained structurally — not detected."* BANNED anywhere: "blocks injection"/"detects malicious intent"/"prevents prompt injection"/"100% blocked". Engine: `qs_normalize` (length-carried, lowercase alnum words, every non-alnum byte → a single-space separator; NEVER strlen'd) → a static human-reviewed table of EMIT-ANCHORED ORDERED phrase patterns — an emission verb (a 23-verb set) + the possessive `your`/a sensitive target within a TIGHT per-pattern gap (up to 4 slots; the canned jailbreak requires adjacency gap-1; first-match-wins, severity-ordered so a combined attack resolves to the most severe class). Audit discipline (`query_shield_reason_label`): a REFUSE records the reason-class LABEL ONLY, never the raw query — so a hostile can't smuggle a blocklist keyword (e.g. "format") into the M3-2 JACT audit; labels are keyword-clean by construction, teeth-proven BOTH ways (label → `shield_assess` EXECUTE + a bounded substring proving the raw query is never inside the label). Corpora: `hostile_queries.h` (20, 1:1 to the patterns + 2 severity-ordered combined) + `benign_queries.h` (100 realistic status/design/dev/count queries — the FP denominator). `test_query_shield.c` (**87 asserts, measured FP = 0/100** printed + hard-fail-if-nonzero — the phase-exit number) + `fuzz_query_shield.c` (300K ASan/UBSan: RAW exact-len-heap + match-preserving-mutation STRUCTURED modes). **Design pre-mortem-hardened by 3 adversarial-review WORKFLOWS** (opus/high; ~33 realistic FPs folded → the systemic bare-possessive→emit-anchor retune; dropped the FP-prone `{key,slot}`/`{action,ids}`/DAN/`{dump,episodic}` rows; the design-opinion gap-4 tighten to gap-2; the 4-slot `{EMIT,your,system,prompt}` for the prompt word-sense). CI: 2 steps (unit links `shield_action.c`+`action_allowlist.c`+`shield_learn.c` for the keyword-clean teeth, `test_monitors` precedent; fuzz is `query_shield.c` only; unit **87 asserts**, fuzz **300,000** iters ASan/UBSan). **M3-1-era status (history): deploy-inert.** **CURRENT: LIVE** — since the 6-5 flip every validated inbound query is scored here before PA will route it, and a refusal is audited by reason-class LABEL only. v11 `control_in_blocked` = a **defined-abuse-class refuse count**, NOT "injection blocked" (the honesty-gate contract); general injection remains contained STRUCTURALLY, not detected. Doc: `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-5_CONTROL_IN.md` (M3-1).
- **Control-IN MODULE INDEX (Phase 6 6-5 — the complete file map; the narrative entries above/below carry the design rationale).** **41 files / 6,733 LOC** (measured 2026-07-21 via `git ls-files` + `wc -l`), **13 CI steps** all green. All of it is LIVE in the deployed image since the flip.
- **Query Router (Phase 6 6-6/B/M0, host):** `phase3/src/ai/route.c/h` — a pure, host-testable keyword intent classifier `route_classify(q,len)` → ROUTE_{SYSFACTS, INFER, DECLINE} (the `query_shield.c` precedent: host-pure, length-carried, NEVER strlen, crash/UB-free for any attacker bytes) + `sysfacts_answer` over a host-WHITELISTED `sysfacts_t` (uptime / boot_id / model / nodes / a coarse health bit ONLY — a `_Static_assert` sizeof+offsetof pins the whitelist; the load-generator counters and all internal/security state are DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED, goal doc §4) + a canned honest DECLINE for no-source status metrics (cpu / disk / wall-clock / temp / memory-used → "I don't track that", NEVER routed to inference to be fabricated). REFUSE stays `query_shield`'s upstream verdict (route_classify runs on QS_ALLOW only). **HONEST CEILING:** a COARSE keyword classifier, NOT semantic — the embedding mechanism is a separate later arc (goal doc §8). **The ≥95% is KEYWORD-BLIND:** `routing_suite.h`'s HELDOUT split was authored by an independent pass with no sight of route.c's keywords; route.c was tuned against the DEV split only and HELDOUT run once → **64/67 = 95.52% on the first run, no iteration; DEV 100%; 0 INFER-misroutes** (the 3 misses are typo probes that fail SAFE to INFER — the honest keyword-router ceiling). **RETUNED 2026-07-23 (`5224a85`) after the supervised flip validation caught a real defect on hardware:** the DECLINE rule matched untracked-metric nouns (cpu/disk/ram/load/temperature/time…) **BARE**, so a CONCEPTUAL question merely containing one was declined — the operator's improvised "why doesn't adding more CPU cores speed up a single-threaded program?" got "I don't track that." That **violated route.h's own conservative rule** (ambiguity → INFER, never toward a wrong answer) and was a **capability REGRESSION** vs the routing-off box. Fix = generalise the guard `memory` already had to every metric noun: DECLINE now requires a **STATUS ANCHOR** (a usage/quantity word, or a possessive/self reference; wall-clock takes its OWN specific anchors so "how much time does quicksort take?" stays conceptual). **Why the suite missed it: every DECLINE case in BOTH splits was a genuine status query, so the DECLINE-vs-INFER boundary was never exercised and 95.52% passed WITH the defect present** — the concrete reason a held-out score is a POINT ESTIMATE, not production accuracy. Suite grew +6 HELDOUT FP cases (authored BEFORE the fix) + 6 distinct DEV ones → **HELDOUT 70/73 = 95.89%, DEV 64/64, 0 INFER misroutes**; the held-out number ROSE while the suite got harder. `test_route.c` 109 asserts + `fuzz_route.c` 300K ASan/UBSan → CI "Phase 6: 6-6/B0 Query router (C)" + "…Fuzz (C, ASAN)". **GOAL 6-6 COMPLETE — `JARVIS_ROUTING` FLIPPED DEFAULT-ON 2026-07-23; `route.c` is LINKED into Process A and called from `pa_ctrl_gate` on every QS_ALLOW control-IN query (see the flag row for the full contract, the shared-exit invariant, the PB-independence property and the OFF-object-identity numbers).** B/M2 = telemetry v12 (routing counts as ADDED fields riding the EXISTING `TLM_F_CONTROL_IN` flag — the u16 flags word is exhausted) + a SEPARATE control-IN-scoped Routing console pane (do NOT merge it with the synthetic-workload aggregate on `Routing.jsx` — two different query populations); then the B flip. Plan: `phase6/docs/PHASE_6_GOAL_6-6_ROUTING.md`.

- **Episodic Store (Phase 5 G1/M0):** **`EPI_ACT_CONTROL_IN_LOCAL = 4` added 2026-07-26 — "an answer the box RENDERED is not a memory".** A control-IN turn that PA answered ITSELF (a 6-6 SYSFACTS reply from live box state, or the canned DECLINE) is tagged 4 instead of 3: still written, still auditable, corpus preserved — simply **never recallable**. **It costs no record-format change** (`action` is already `uint16`; the record stays exactly 512 B and the one-sector `_Static_assert` is untouched) and it is an **append-only enum bump**, the established pattern (`MON_EV_DEGRADED`, `ACTION_STATUS_DIGEST`). **It works AT THE BOOT SCAN, which is the hard half** — the scan rebuilds the index from stored bytes and cannot know what route a turn took, but the tag is STORED. **A FAILED turn (`EPI_OUT_ERROR`) stays tag 3 whatever its route** — it has no answer at all, and tagging it 4 would conflate "answered from its own state" with "failed to answer". **ONE CORRECTION TO THE "excluded for free" CLAIM, found by reading rather than assuming: that was true of the FETCH filter only.** `g3_candidate_usable` keys on `== EPI_ACT_CONTROL_IN` so it rejects tag 4 automatically — but the two **INDEX** predicates (`ctrl_epi_write` + the boot scan) checked only `outcome`/`resp_len` and **not `action`**, because every record in that store used to be tag 3. Left alone, a tag-4 record would have been INDEXED and then rejected at fetch, and since `epi_index_lookup` is newest-wins with no fallback it would **SHADOW the good prior answer under that key** — the exact defect the existing comment warns about, and **live**: the 6-6 false-positive question was DECLINEd before `5224a85` and is INFERred after, so both tags exist under one key. Fixed by making **both index sites CALL `g3_candidate_usable`** instead of restating it, so "index and filter cannot disagree" is now true **by construction rather than by comment**. `phase3/src/ai/episodic_store.c/h` — raw-LBA circular 512 B-record store (nvme_log pattern, struct+callback-driven so the later semantic/SHIELD stores reuse the core; no device dependency — M1 wires the NVMe callbacks). Record = `{boot_id, seq, t_ms, query_key, action, outcome, feedback, query, resp}`; `query_key = cache_hash(cache_normalize_query(query))` (decision-cache FNV-1a parity for #6 cache-growth). Host-tested `test_episodic_store.c` (7/7: fresh-init, append+read-back round-trip, boot_id increment, checksum corruption, wrap+migration, key-parity, batched-fill) → CI step "Phase 5: Episodic Store (C)". **M1 (2026-06-27):** `episodic_fill` (fill-only) split out, `epi_store_append` now stamps boot_id/seq at write time; wired into Process A (`main_x86.c`) — `epi_batch_add` fills a RAM batch per cache/inference query, `epi_commit` flushes to NVMe (via `epi_nvme_read/write` bounce) at the [STATS] cadence (gated on `g_episodic_ready`); `build_jarvis_x86.sh` compiles it into the Process-A source list. Box build + QEMU/KVM smoke pending (no host compile of `main_x86.c`). **M1a (2026-06-27):** episodic init decoupled from nvme_log (was nested in its success block → never ran when nvme_log_init fails, incl. the 12 G QEMU image); now gated only on g_nvme_ptr/bounce. Box smoke re-run pending. **M2 (2026-06-27):** `parse_episodic.py` (reads the raw region from a file/`dd` pipe, validates the JEPI header XOR, wrap-aware, decodes `epi_record_t`) + `test_parse_episodic.py` C->Python round-trip (C `--dump` fixed fixture → parse → field-exact + FNV-1a key parity, teeth-proven) → CI step "Phase 5: Episodic Parser round-trip (Python)". Canon done-when "episodic log readable/parseable" met (host); box-real `dd` parse pending. **M3 (2026-06-27):** reboot-survival proven via QEMU 2-boot proxy — boot_id 1→2, 165 prior records intact + 165 new appended, err=0; real-box power-cycle PROVEN ON HARDWARE (JARVIS PC, /dev/nvme0n1 @ LBA 21,100,000, hard power-cycles): boot 1 committed 335 records (cache + Gemma inference), boot 2 re-init read the persisted 335 + appended 84 (total 419, no clobber), boot 3 re-init read 419 + bumped boot_id→3 (no new commit — power-cycled before the q=100 cadence); 419/419 cursor/total, read back coherent from Ubuntu via `sudo dd ... skip=21100000 count=8193 | parse_episodic.py` (header valid, real XOR checksum OK, magic JEPI). **M4 (2026-06-27):** episodic_count surfaced on the read-only console — telemetry reserved2 renamed to episodic_count + TLM_F_MEMORY 0x20 (NO packet-size bump; CRC@196 unchanged), decoded by telemetry_receiver, golden fixtures regenerated, Capabilities "Episodic memory store" row + System "Episodic records" stat; host/CI green (telemetry layout/CRC + key-contract + golden-drift + honesty + e2e), CONFIRMED ON HARDWARE — episodic_count climbed live 335→419 + TLM_F_MEMORY rendered the console "Episodic memory store" Capabilities row + "Episodic records" System stat over the real I211 NIC (not QEMU), NN=6, err=0. **G1 (episodic store) M0–M4 COMPLETE.**

M4a/b/c CI-green + box-confirmed; M4d (live v3 telemetry over the I211) batchable with M6. **M5a (2026-06-30): NVMe-backed post-reboot recall.** `epi_index_lookup` (pure, host-tested — `test_episodic_store.c` **8/8 as of G3/M5a**: miss/empty→-1, single hit, dup→newest logical_index — the suite is **10 PASS** today, +T9 region-isolation and +T10 recallable-only-index). At boot PA builds an in-RAM `key→logical_index` map of the persisted episodic store (ONE-time scan); LANE B retrieval, after the in-RAM batch, does an O(1)-ish lookup + ONE bounded `epi_store_read` of the matched record (dedup: this-boot batch wins if it already has the key; guards: read-key==qkey + `g3_candidate_usable`, so a stale index after a commit or a persisted CACHE record can't pollute) — so a fact stored in a PRIOR boot recalls after a power-cycle. `[RETR]` gains `recall=` (1 = preamble fact came from the persisted store). All gated `JARVIS_G3_RETRIEVAL` default-off → deploy byte-identical; the recall sequence is host-verified against the real modules (scratch 10/10: reboot-survival + exact-hit + dedup/usable guards). seL4-only → the ≥10-fact power-cycle recall is the M5b box smoke. **M5 BOX-PROVEN 2026-06-30:** `[RECALL] index n=330` built at boot, 6 distinct queries recalled their prior-boot records (`recall=1`), ~0.5 ms bounded fetch — post-reboot recall works end-to-end (G3 retrieval done-when's recall half closed). **M6 SHIPPED (2026-07-02): `JARVIS_G3_RETRIEVAL` default-ON — retrieval DEPLOYED.** The flip-to-production-default was a product call gated on an **offline OFF-vs-ON A/B**; three A/Bs cleared it (`JARVIS_G3_AB` harness `9ba7007`, deterministic `rng_state=42` paired OFF/ON KVM runs). **A/B1** (2026-07-01, OFF=22/ON=10): net-neutral-to-slightly-positive (blind 3-2), NOT harmful, but one hard failure — **P6:** the injected preamble echoed a topically-distant fact into an seL4 answer; root cause = `g3_select`'s recency fallback + `g3_build_preamble` embedding the prior QUESTION text. **P6 fix `70ca236`:** LANE B now uses `g3_select_exact_only` (exact-key match only — **recency fallback DROPPED for injection; do not re-add**) + `g3_build_preamble_answer_only` (**fenced answer-only preamble, no embedded prior-question text**); `g3_select`/`g3_build_preamble` kept unchanged for non-injection use. **A/B2** (on `70ca236`): **net-positive (ON 4/7 blind) + contamination-free**, but exposed **P7** (self-echo on exact repeats: a dangling "### W" markdown-header fragment from mid-token truncation + verbatim lead-sentence restatement). **P7 fix `2bb537b`:** `g3_clean_answer_len` clean-boundary truncation (cut at the last complete word + strip a dangling markdown header) + the "Notes from a previous answer (use as reference; add new detail, do not repeat)" build-on label; `test_g3_retrieval.c` **53 PASS** (T8 exact-only, T9 anti-echo, T10 clean-truncation). **A/B3** (2026-07-02, on `2bb537b`, OFF=22/ON=13): **ALL CLEAN** — `### W`=0, `[RETR] hit=1`×14, zero contamination, zero label leakage, coherent, err=0; blind panel (13 pairs × 3 lenses + artifact/progression analysts) net-neutral-to-slightly-positive, **zero materially-worse cases** (greedy OFF gives a repeat-asker byte-identical output, so ON ≥ OFF everywhere; clear ON wins on the build-on families). The residual lead-sentence overlap on repeats is the **model's natural greedy answer** (OFF produces the identical opening with no injection) — not an echo defect; the first-sentence-only excerpt follow-up was evaluated and NOT taken. The per-milestone "default OFF / deploy byte-identical" notes above describe the pre-M6 history; **the deployed image is now intentionally not byte-identical to v1.0.0** (retrieval ships ON; hits+latency are the metrics, "memory helped" is never a system claim). **M4d BOX-VERIFIED LIVE 2026-07-03 (boot_id=11):** live I211 v3 telemetry (34 packets crc_ok, `TLM_F_RETRIEVAL`, `retrieval_hits` 1→2→3, `retrieval_latency_us` 192→5078 µs, err=0, coherent, `episodic_count=584`) + the console retrieval row rendering live — **G3 COMPLETE (M0–M6)**. **#6 cache-growth: route-through-cache (`7e8c30f`) REVERTED** (wrong design vs canon) — canon = **promote repeated query→action patterns from the episodic log** into the decision cache (plan: `phase5/docs/PHASE_5_GOAL6_CACHE_GROWTH.md`, authored plan-first); the `JARVIS_CACHE_GROWTH` flag is **retained** (the canon promotion pass reuses it).
- **Cache Growth (Phase 5 #6/M0+M1):** `phase3/src/ai/cache_growth.c/h` — pure-C promotion **selector** (`cg_select_promotions`: counts `query_key` frequency over a scanned episodic window, promotes keys with frequency ≥ `CG_PROMOTE_THRESHOLD`=2, emits the **newest** candidate per key, dedups by key; borrowed text carried by pointer + `*_len`, never `strlen`'d — no seL4/device/`decision_cache` dep) + **(M1) the Option-B rolling frequency aggregate** (`cg_freq_slot_t[CG_FREQ_CAP=1024]` open-addressed linear-probe table, `count==0`=empty sentinel; `cg_freq_bump` inserts-or-increments and returns the new count (0 if full+absent), `cg_freq_get` probes-to-empty — pure, closes the SYSTEM_DESIGN "frequency-signal gap": the M5 recall index is deduped-to-newest and can't count). Frequency-based + deterministic — the cache **learns frequently-asked queries and serves them fast**, never "understands". Host-tested `test_cache_growth.c` (**22 PASS**: T1–T5 threshold/dedup/newest-wins/n=0/max-cap + M1 T6–T9 bump/get, independent counts, collision probing, saturation) → CI step "Phase 5: Cache-Growth promotion logic (C)". **The dormant SEC-024 LRU eviction goes LIVE + tested** (`decision_cache.c:214-238`, the phase3 copy's fill-then-evict path, unreached at ~308/512): `test_decision_cache_lru.c` (**10/10**: fill to `CACHE_SIZE`=512 → access-control the LRU → insert a 513th key → oldest-accessed evicted, `entries_used` stays 512, new key present, recently-accessed survivors kept) → CI step "Phase 5: Decision-cache LRU eviction (C)". **M1 (box wiring, 2026-07-03):** Process A (`main_x86.c`, all `#if JARVIS_CACHE_GROWTH`) seeds the aggregate at the boot recall-scan (outer gate now `(JARVIS_G3_RETRIEVAL || JARVIS_CACHE_GROWTH)`), snapshots `g_cache_baseline` after pattern load, and at the `[STATS]` cadence (BEFORE `epi_commit` clears the batch) folds the batch then promotes usable INFER answers (`EPI_ACT_INFER`+`EPI_OUT_OK`+`resp_len>0`, freq≥2, newest-per-key, normalize-or-skip, resp clamped to `MAX_ACTION_LEN-1`) via `cache_insert` (update-in-place = idempotent; a promotion counts only when `entries_used` grows) under `CG_PROMOTE_HWM` (409 = 0.8×512, §7 — EMPTY slots survive; SEC-024 LRU stays a guardrail); `[CACHE-GROW] promoted=/used=/grow=/hwm=` proof line; `build_jarvis_x86.sh` syncs + injects `cache_growth.c` into the PA source list. Gated `JARVIS_CACHE_GROWTH` default-0 (transient sed flip for the M1 smoke; default flips at M3). Design: `phase5/docs/PHASE_5_GOAL6_CACHE_GROWTH.md`.

Keys = episodic `query_key` (FNV-1a parity — the deliberate mapping change from Phase-1's action_type). **MONITOR-ONLY (D-c): learns + surfaces, NEVER blocks — SEC-039 unchanged; enforcement is Phase 6.** Persistence = D-a derive-from-episodic (ERROR/BLOCKED records; boot-scan seed + batch fold at M1 — no second NVMe store). Design: `phase5/docs/PHASE_5_GOAL5_SHIELD_LEARNING.md`.
- **Semantic Memory (Phase 5 #4/M0):** `phase3/src/ai/semantic_store.c/h` — a SEPARATE raw-LBA circular **fact store** (the `episodic_store` clone: header + 512 B records, XOR checksum, boot_id, circular cursor + monotonic total, flush-after-write, device-independent callbacks) at `SEM_STORE_BASE_LBA` **21,110,000** (+4097 sectors ≈ 2 MiB, clear of episodic which ends @ 21,108,192; RESERVED like the episodic region), magic "JSEM", `semantic_fact_t` = `{boot_id, seq, t_ms, key, fact_type, support_count, confidence_x100, text_len, text[440]}` (exactly 512 B) — **plus `sem_store_upsert`**: insert-or-raise-support (a repeated subject UPDATES its fact — text/t_ms follow the newest, `support_count = max(existing, incoming)` = monotonic + **idempotent across boot re-distills**, seq keeps identity; never a dup). `phase3/src/ai/semantic_distill.c/h` — the pure **DETERMINISTIC distill** (`sd_distill`, **#7 consolidation's compact-core, landed here**): group `epi_record_t[]` by `query_key`, support = usable records only (`sd_record_usable` = INFER+OK+resp>0, the #6/G3 filter — cache echoes/failures never become "facts"), emit the NEWEST usable answer per key at `support >= SEM_MIN_SUPPORT` (=3); `confidence_x100` = % of usable same-key answers byte-identical to the chosen one; text copied by `resp_len` never strlen. **NO LLM, NO embeddings (PLAN §7.2/§7.6 — Phase 7); honest ceiling = "compacts what it has repeatedly seen" (observable patterns), NEVER "knows preferences"/"understands".** #4 = store + distill only; the G3 retrieval hook is a future slice; **#4/#7 are completeness/Phase-6-readiness goals — Phase 5's canonical done-when is already met.** Host-tested `test_semantic_store.c` (**6 PASS**: fresh-init/layout, round-trip + stamps, reboot-bump persist, corrupt-header, wrap, upsert-monotonic) + `test_semantic_distill.c` (**8 PASS**: threshold, support + FNV-1a key parity, newest-wins + honest confidence, dedup, guards, max-cap, usable filter, length-not-strlen) → CI steps "Phase 5: Semantic store (C)" + "Phase 5: Semantic distill (C)". **M1 (box wiring, 2026-07-04, BOX-VERIFIED):** gated `JARVIS_SEMANTIC` (jarvis_debug.h, **default 0**) in Process A — `sem_store_init(&g_semantic, epi_nvme_read/write, …)` right after episodic-ready; the boot recall-scan buffers the newest ≤`SEM_DISTILL_WINDOW`(1024) records tail-only/chronological (no ring — `sd_distill`'s newest-wins is position-based) then runs the one-shot distill + upserts with a `[SEM] window=/facts=/upserted=/stored=` proof line; `build_jarvis_x86.sh` syncs + injects both .c's into the PA source list. **WRITE-ONLY at M1** — the store is never read in inference/routing. S0-snapshot OFF-vs-ON smoke: ON `[SEM] window=1024 facts=1 upserted=1 stored=1`, OFF 0×`[SEM]`, `[INFER]` byte-identical (16=16), err=0. Design: `phase5/docs/PHASE_5_GOAL4_SEMANTIC_MEMORY.md`.

- **Model-load fail-closed (Phase C / C/M1b pre-fix, 2026-07-25, UNGATED deployed path):** two detected-then-DISCARDED failures on the model-load path used to let the box serve inference from an unusable model while telemetry still reported `model=loaded`. (a) `main_x86.c` frame-alloc: on exhaustion `n_pages` truncated but `model_size` did NOT, and the read still wrote the full size into the shorter mapping — the `alloc_fail` guard was **set and never read** (a dead variable). (b) `spawn_inference_process`'s mapping loop: all three failure branches did `merr++; continue;`, printed a count, and continued with a **partially mapped model** — PB then ran on garbage weights, i.e. fluent nonsense asserted with confidence. Both now FAIL CLOSED via a `g_model_bad` latch: the frame-alloc case **skips the read entirely** (no overflow, `nvme_model_loaded` stays 0), the map case latches after the loop (**propagation note:** the map runs in `spawn_inference_process`, a different function from the PA load path, and its return is treated as fatal by the caller — so it latches the file-scope flag instead of returning an error, which would newly brick a boot that used to survive); `TLM_F_MODEL_LOADED` is CLEARED on the wire; inference dispatch is refused by folding `!g_model_bad` into the **same lane condition as the §5-F `PB_DISPATCH_OK()` gate** (so `q_infer` is never inflated and the §5-F "no send / no timeout / no q_error / no miss churn" semantics are inherited verbatim); and a durable `[MODEL-BAD] inference disabled why= detail= q_err=` line rides `nvme_log_write` at the `[STATS]` cadence so the proof survives `BOOT_LOG=0`. **Deliberately NOT fatal to boot** — a shipped system with no observed failures must degrade, not stop (the `g_pb_dead` precedent). **HUD/`[SNAP]` model status now reports USABILITY (2026-07-25 follow-up):** the status token was two-state and rendered from `nvme_model_loaded` alone, which is TRUTHFULLY 1 in the partial-map case (the file read fine; the MAP failed) — so it read `model=loaded` next to a `[MODEL-BAD]` line. Not a silent health claim (they render adjacently), but confusing at a glance and contrary to the UI-shows-real-state rule. Now three states in one glanceable token: `loading` (not read yet) / `loaded` (read AND usable — the healthy string is UNCHANGED) / **`UNUSABLE`** (read but `g_model_bad`; inference refused). The on-screen FB panel, which wrote `[loaded]` on the read-success path BEFORE the map runs, is corrected once at the first `[STATS]` window (`fb_status_line` is a static defined later in the file, so it is not callable from the failure site). The load-bearing signals — cleared `TLM_F_MODEL_LOADED` and refused dispatch — are untouched. KVM-proven both ways: degraded run = `model=UNUSABLE` on all 1912 `[SNAP]` lines with zero `loaded`; healthy run = `model=loaded` with zero `UNUSABLE` and zero `MODEL-BAD`. **Honest limit: the FB panel correction is NOT KVM-provable** — QEMU reports `disp=no-fb`, so `fb_ready()` is false and that line never renders; it is bare-metal-only and remains unproven until a box boot. **NO object-identity claim is available or made** — this is an ungated deployed-path change with no OFF state; the honest gate is a behaviourally identical healthy boot **plus** a demonstrated failure branch (both met — see the `JARVIS_MODEL_FAIL_PROBE` row). CI: **N/A** — `main_x86.c` is seL4-only and never host-compiled, so no CI step was added rather than adding one that cannot run.
- **x86 Rootserver:** `phase3/src/sel4/main_x86.c` — **Phase 6 K/M1 (2026-07-04):** gated `#if JARVIS_ACTIONS` action-audit-store init (`act_audit_init`, nvme_log-independent, after episodic-ready) + the one-shot `#if JARVIS_ACTION_PROBE` induced-BLOCK probe (three cases through the linked `shield_action` gate, placed after the boot `[RECALL]`/`[SHIELD-LEARN]` seed scan so case C uses the live `g_shield_learn` map; NOTHING executes — M1 proves the gate REFUSES before M2 executes anything). Flag-OFF compiles the includes + store + probe out → deployed path byte-identical + action-inert.
- **PB response-chunk send — SILENT-DROP FIXED 2026-07-26 (ungated, `inference_server.c`):** the chunk loop did `(void)rc` then `offset += chunk` **regardless**, while `shmem_ipc_send` returns **−1 on a full ring** (`shmem_ipc.c`: `wr - rd >= size`). A dropped chunk therefore punched a **SILENT HOLE** in the answer — PA reassembled text with a gap, no error, no log. Latent only because `text_out[512]` capped a response at ≤3 chunks into a 15-slot ring; the M2 transport work raises exactly those ceilings, so the branch was proven BEFORE it became reachable. **Fix = RETRY THE SAME OFFSET with back-pressure** (signal `resp_notif` + `seL4_Yield` so PA — which polls this ring and shares the core — drains), bounded by `PB_SEND_RETRY_MAX` 256; **the offset advances ONLY after a send that succeeded**, so an answer can be short but is always **CONTIGUOUS, never holed**. Chosen over fail-and-mark because a full ring here is TRANSIENT and retrying PRESERVES the answer, whereas marking degraded would need a new message type/field for a case the retry makes nearly unreachable. On exhaustion it stops and logs loudly via **`puts_serial`, NOT `pb_log`** — `pb_log` sends MSG_DEBUG over the very ring that is full. **BOTH BRANCHES GATED under KVM via `JARVIS_RING_PROBE` (default 0), a branch that had never executed:** mode 1 (fail the first 3 sends) → retry recovered, **0 truncation lines**, 14 coherent `[INFER]` incl. the first response whose chunks were all forced to fail, `err=0`; mode 2 (fail every send) → `[PB] RESPONSE TRUNCATED: response ring full after 256 retries, undelivered bytes=263 (answer is short but CONTIGUOUS — no hole)`, **0 FATAL** (degrades, never crashes).
